---EXIT POLICY---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: /**
   8:  * Shared production/replay exit authority for an already-open long position.
   9:  *
  10:  * The policy deliberately separates short-timeframe noise from a genuine
  11:  * higher-timeframe breakdown:
  12:  *  - a 1m pullback alone never closes the position;
  13:  *  - the existing 1m SELL + bearish 5m confirmation remains valid;
  14:  *  - a fresh 1h SELL may close a position when 5m is no longer supportive;
  15:  *  - a Profit Lock breach is tolerated while 5m/1h still support the bullish thesis.
  16:  */
  17: @Component
  18: public class PositionExitPolicy {
  19: 
  20:     public Evaluation evaluateNormalExit(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  21:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  22:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  23:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  24: 
  25:         boolean oneHourBreakdown = bearish(oneDecision)
  26:                 && fiveMinute != null
  27:                 && !supportive(fiveDecision);
  28:         if (oneHourBreakdown) {
  29:             return Evaluation.exit("HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED",
  30:                     "1h turned " + label(oneDecision)
  31:                             + " while 5m is no longer supportive (" + label(fiveDecision)
  32:                             + "). The higher-timeframe bullish thesis is broken; close the managed position.");
  33:         }
  34: 
  35:         boolean lowerFrameConfirmed = current != null
  36:                 && "1m".equals(current.getInterval())
  37:                 && bearish(currentDecision)
  38:                 && fiveMinute != null
  39:                 && bearish(fiveDecision)
  40:                 && (oneHour == null || !bullish(oneDecision));
  41:         if (lowerFrameConfirmed) {
  42:             return Evaluation.exit("SELL_CONFIRMED",
  43:                     "1m " + label(currentDecision) + " is confirmed by bearish 5m while 1h is not bullish.");
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return Evaluation.hold("No confirmed multi-timeframe exit."
  47:                 + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  48:                 + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  49:                 + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     public Evaluation evaluateProfitLockBreach(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour) {
  53:         SignalDecision currentDecision = decision(current);
  54:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = decision(fiveMinute);
  55:         SignalDecision oneDecision = decision(oneHour);
  56: 
  57:         // Strong HTF support: tolerate a sharp 1m pullback instead of throwing away the trend.
  58:         if (bullish(oneDecision) && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)) {
  59:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  60:                     "Profit Lock was breached, but higher-timeframe continuation remains healthy:"
  61:                             + " 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  62:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  63:                             + ". Treat the breach as a protected pullback, not an immediate SELL.");
  64:         }
  65: 
  66:         // Cooling but still-supported trend: WATCH/WATCH is allowed to breathe while the current frame is not bearish.
  67:         if (oneDecision == SignalDecision.WATCH
  68:                 && fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveDecision)
  69:                 && !bearish(currentDecision)) {
  70:             return Evaluation.hold("PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD",
  71:                     "Profit Lock was breached during a supported consolidation:"
  72:                             + " 1h=WATCH, 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  73:                             + ", current=" + label(currentDecision)
  74:                             + ". Keep the winner protected while waiting for higher-timeframe confirmation.");
  75:         }
  76: 
  77:         if (bearish(oneDecision)) {
  78:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_HTF_EXIT",
  79:                     "Profit Lock was breached and 1h is " + label(oneDecision)
  80:                             + "; higher-timeframe support has failed.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         if (bearish(currentDecision) && bearish(fiveDecision)) {
  84:             return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_BEARISH_CONFIRMATION",
  85:                     "Profit Lock was breached with bearish 1m and 5m confirmation.");
  86:         }
  87: 
  88:         return Evaluation.exit("PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT",
  89:                 "Profit Lock was breached without enough higher-timeframe bullish support to justify a protected hold:"
  90:                         + " current=" + label(currentDecision)
  91:                         + ", 5m=" + label(fiveDecision)
  92:                         + ", 1h=" + label(oneDecision) + ".");
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private SignalDecision decision(TradeSignal signal) {
  96:         return signal == null ? null : signal.getDecision();
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision decision) {
 100:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY
 101:                 || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY
 102:                 || decision == SignalDecision.WATCH;
 103:     }
 104: 
 105:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 106:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 107:     }
 108: 
 109:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 110:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     private String label(SignalDecision decision) {
 114:         return decision == null ? "MISSING" : decision.name();
 115:     }
 116: 
 117:     public record Evaluation(boolean exit, String code, String explanation) {
 118:         public static Evaluation exit(String code, String explanation) {
 119:             return new Evaluation(true, code, explanation);
 120:         }
 121: 
 122:         public static Evaluation hold(String explanation) {
 123:             return new Evaluation(false, "HOLD", explanation);
 124:         }
 125: 
 126:         public static Evaluation hold(String code, String explanation) {
 127:             return new Evaluation(false, code, explanation);
 128:         }
 129:     }
 130: }
---CONTINUATION POLICY---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Shared production/replay policy for deciding whether a reached profit target should be extended.
  10:  *
  11:  * IMPORTANT: this class manages an ALREADY-OPEN winner only. It does not loosen BUY generation,
  12:  * entry validation, stop loss, or bearish timeframe vetoes.
  13:  */
  14: @Component
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class PositionContinuationPolicy {
  17:     /** Minor/moderate immutable-thesis deterioration is tolerated at a TP checkpoint. */
  18:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE = 2;
  19:     /** Momentum must remain essentially intact for the thesis-preservation continuation path. */
  20:     static final int MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE = 1;
  21: 
  22:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
  23: 
  24:     /**
  25:      * Backward-compatible evaluator used by older unit fixtures. Production/replay should pass
  26:      * the full immutable entry thesis through the overload below.
  27:      */
  28:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  29:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume) {
  30:         return evaluate(current, fiveMinute, oneHour,
  31:                 entryTrend, null, entryMomentum, entryVolume, null, null);
  32:     }
  33: 
  34:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal current, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  35:                                Integer entryTrend, Integer entryStructure,
  36:                                Integer entryMomentum, Integer entryVolume,
  37:                                Integer entryConfidence, Integer entryTotal) {
  38:         if (current == null) return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú no fresh signal context.");
  39:         if (bearish(current.getDecision()) || (fiveMinute != null && bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision()))
  40:                 || (oneHour != null && bearish(oneHour.getDecision()))) {
  41:             return new Evaluation(false, "Continuation FAIL Aú bearish timeframe detected; normal exit remains active.");
  42:         }
  43: 
  44:         boolean currentSupport = supportive(current.getDecision());
  45:         boolean fiveSupport = fiveMinute != null && supportive(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  46:         boolean fiveNonBearish = fiveMinute != null && !bearish(fiveMinute.getDecision());
  47:         boolean oneSafe = oneHour == null || !bearish(oneHour.getDecision());
  48:         boolean oneBullish = oneHour != null && bullish(oneHour.getDecision());
  49: 
  50:         int trendFloor = Math.max(12, nvl(entryTrend) - 3);
  51:         int momentumFloor = Math.max(7, nvl(entryMomentum) - 4);
  52:         int volumeSoftFloor = Math.max(2, nvl(entryVolume) - 8);
  53: 
  54:         boolean trendHealthy = current.getTrendScore() >= trendFloor;
  55:         boolean momentumHealthy = current.getMomentumScore() >= momentumFloor;
  56:         boolean volumeSupportive = current.getVolumeScore() >= volumeSoftFloor;
  57: 
  58:         // Standard continuation: structure is still supportive. Volume is deliberately a soft
  59:         // confirmation because breakout volume commonly cools while a healthy trend continues.
  60:         boolean standard = currentSupport && fiveSupport && oneSafe
  61:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy
  62:                 && (volumeSupportive || oneBullish);
  63: 
  64:         // Protected HTF continuation: a live 1h BUY may carry a winner through a neutral/cooling
  65:         // 5m phase. It never overrides bearish context and still requires healthy 1m trend/momentum.
  66:         boolean htfProtected = currentSupport && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish
  67:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  68: 
  69:         // Healthy consolidation: low volume is deliberately NOT a hard veto for an already
  70:         // profitable position. If 1m is still WATCH/BUY, the higher timeframes are non-bearish,
  71:         // and trend/momentum remain healthy, cooling volume alone must not force a TP exit.
  72:         boolean healthyConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  73:                 && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  74: 
  75:         // HTF-supported consolidation: a strong 1h BUY may carry a winner through one neutral
  76:         // 1m candle when 5m remains non-bearish. This is position-management-only; supportive()
  77:         // is intentionally unchanged so entry rules are not loosened.
  78:         boolean currentNonBearish = !bearish(current.getDecision());
  79:         boolean htfSupportedConsolidation = currentNonBearish && !currentSupport
  80:                 && oneBullish && fiveNonBearish && trendHealthy && momentumHealthy;
  81: 
  82:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  83:                 entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  84:                 entryConfidence, entryTotal, current);
  85: 
  86:         // FIX-011 / SOLUSDT 2026-08-19:
  87:         // At TP, SOL had 1m WATCH, 5m NEUTRAL, 1h WATCH and momentum improved 13->15.
  88:         // The old binary trend floor rejected continuation solely because trend fell 21->16
  89:         // (16 < 18), even though PositionManagementService scored the SAME immutable thesis as
  90:         // trend pressure 2/8, momentum pressure 0/5 and HOLD. This narrow path aligns both
  91:         // components: minor thesis cooling may extend a winner, but any bearish timeframe still
  92:         // vetoes above and genuine trend/momentum breakdown still fails these pressure limits.
  93:         boolean thesisIntactConsolidation = currentSupport && fiveNonBearish && oneSafe
  94:                 && thesis.trendPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_TREND_PRESSURE
  95:                 && thesis.momentumPressure() <= MAX_CONTINUATION_MOMENTUM_PRESSURE;
  96: 
  97:         boolean extend = standard || htfProtected || healthyConsolidation
  98:                 || htfSupportedConsolidation || thesisIntactConsolidation;
  99:         String path = htfProtected ? "HTF_TREND"
 100:                 : (standard ? "STANDARD"
 101:                 : (htfSupportedConsolidation ? "HTF_SUPPORTED_CONSOLIDATION"
 102:                 : (healthyConsolidation ? "HEALTHY_CONSOLIDATION"
 103:                 : (thesisIntactConsolidation ? "THESIS_INTACT_CONSOLIDATION" : "NONE"))));
 104:         String checks = "current=" + decision(current)
 105:                 + ", 5m=" + decision(fiveMinute)
 106:                 + ", 1h=" + decision(oneHour)
 107:                 + ", trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + "/" + trendFloor
 108:                 + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "/" + momentumFloor
 109:                 + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore() + "/soft " + volumeSoftFloor
 110:                 + ", thesisPressure=" + thesis.trendPressure() + "/8 trend, "
 111:                 + thesis.momentumPressure() + "/5 momentum";
 112: 
 113:         return new Evaluation(extend, extend
 114:                 ? "Continuation PASS (" + path + "): " + checks
 115:                     + ". Volume is advisory during a healthy winner; cooling volume alone does not end the trend."
 116:                 : "Continuation FAIL: " + checks
 117:                     + ". Trend/momentum or higher-timeframe structure no longer supports extending the target; allow normal protection/exit logic.");
 118:     }
 119: 
 120:     private int nvl(Integer v) { return v == null ? 0 : v; }
 121:     private String decision(TradeSignal s) { return s == null || s.getDecision() == null ? "MISSING" : s.getDecision().name(); }
 122:     private boolean supportive(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY || d == SignalDecision.WATCH; }
 123:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY; }
 124:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
 125:     public record Evaluation(boolean extendTarget, String explanation) {}
 126: }
---PROFIT LOCK 1-180---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletManagedPosition;
   5: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletSettings;
   6: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
   7: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletSettingsRepository;
   8: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   9: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  11: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  12: 
  13: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  14: import java.math.RoundingMode;
  15: import java.time.Instant;
  16: import java.util.Locale;
  17: 
  18: /**
  19:  * Position-manager profit protection for already-open long positions.
  20:  *
  21:  * The BUY/SELL analysis engine is deliberately not involved here. The lock is
  22:  * derived only from the immutable entry price/take-profit stored on the wallet
  23:  * position and the best price observed after entry.
  24:  *
  25:  * Adaptive policy:
  26:  *  - derive an entry-quality score from the immutable entry signal score and confidence;
  27:  *  - stronger setups receive more room before protection activates;
  28:  *  - weaker/medium setups protect profits earlier;
  29:  *  - the protected price can only move upward and never loosens after activation.
  30:  *
  31:  * Administration values remain the fallback when entry-quality information is unavailable.
  32:  */
  33: @Service
  34: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  35: @Slf4j
  36: public class DynamicProfitLockService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  39:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  40: 
  41:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository positionRepository;
  42:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  43:     private final ProfitLockPolicy profitLockPolicy;
  44: 
  45:     @Transactional
  46:     public Evaluation evaluate(TradeSignal signal) {
  47:         if (signal == null) {
  48:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  49:         }
  50:         return evaluatePrice(signal.getSymbol(), signal.getLatestPrice());
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     /**
  54:      * Evaluates Dynamic Profit Lock directly from a live market price.
  55:      * This path is used by live position protection between candle-close signals.
  56:      *
  57:      * FIX-118 behavioral phase:
  58:      * TP_EXTENSION_REBASE preserves the historically earned lock in persistence but makes
  59:      * it non-executable until progress against the CURRENT TP reaches the configured
  60:      * activation threshold again. This gives an approved continuation meaningful room
  61:      * without forgetting that profit protection had already been earned.
  62:      */
  63:     @Transactional
  64:     public Evaluation evaluatePrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal currentPrice) {
  65:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || currentPrice == null
  66:                 || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  67:             return Evaluation.inactive("No valid market price is available.");
  68:         }
  69: 
  70:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  71:         WalletManagedPosition position = positionRepository
  72:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  73:                 .orElse(null);
  74:         if (position == null) {
  75:             return Evaluation.inactive("No open wallet position exists.");
  76:         }
  77: 
  78:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  79:         if (!settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled()) {
  80:             return Evaluation.inactive("Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled in Administration.");
  81:         }
  82: 
  83:         BigDecimal entry = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
  84:         BigDecimal target = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
  85:         BigDecimal current = currentPrice;
  86:         if (entry == null || target == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  87:             return Evaluation.inactive("The position does not have a valid long take-profit distance.");
  88:         }
  89: 
  90:         ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings);
  91:         BigDecimal activation = profile.activationPercent();
  92:         BigDecimal initialLock = profile.initialLockPercent();
  93:         BigDecimal trailStep = profile.trailStepPercent();
  94:         BigDecimal previousHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
  95:         boolean previousPersistedActive = position.isProfitLockActive();
  96:         BigDecimal previousLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
  97:         BigDecimal previousProgress = position.getProfitLockProgressPercent();
  98:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
  99: 
 100:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest == null ? current : previousHighest.max(current);
 101:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(entry, target, highest);
 102:         BigDecimal lockPrice = previousLock;
 103:         ProfitLockState state = previousState;
 104:         boolean executableActive;
 105:         boolean triggered;
 106: 
 107:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 108:             // Keep the legacy active flag/lock persisted so history is not lost, but do not
 109:             // allow the superseded geometry to trigger an exit during the rebase window.
 110:             executableActive = false;
 111:             triggered = false;
 112:         } else {
 113:             // FIX-118 refinement: when a TP-extension rebase earns activation against the
 114:             // CURRENT target, rebuild the executable lock from the current geometry instead
 115:             // of carrying the historical pre-extension lock through ProfitLockPolicy's
 116:             // monotonic previous-lock rule.
 117:             boolean freshGeometryActivation = previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 118:             ProfitLockPolicy.State policyState = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 119:                     entry, target, current, highest,
 120:                     freshGeometryActivation ? false : (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || previousPersistedActive),
 121:                     freshGeometryActivation ? null : previousLock,
 122:                     true, activation, initialLock, trailStep);
 123:             highest = policyState.highestPrice();
 124:             progress = policyState.progressPercent();
 125:             lockPrice = policyState.lockPrice();
 126:             executableActive = policyState.active();
 127:             triggered = policyState.triggered();
 128:             state = executableActive ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 129:         }
 130: 
 131:         // During REBASE the legacy flag intentionally remains true: protection was historically
 132:         // earned. The explicit state is now the sole execution authority.
 133:         boolean persistedActive = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 134:                 ? true
 135:                 : executableActive;
 136: 
 137:         Instant activatedAt = position.getProfitLockActivatedAt();
 138:         if (persistedActive && activatedAt == null) activatedAt = Instant.now();
 139:         Instant rebaseStartedAt = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 140:                 ? position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt()
 141:                 : null;
 142: 
 143:         boolean changed = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 144:                 || highest.compareTo(position.getHighestPriceUsdt()) != 0
 145:                 || position.isProfitLockActive() != persistedActive
 146:                 || different(position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(), lockPrice)
 147:                 || different(position.getProfitLockProgressPercent(), progress)
 148:                 || position.getProfitLockState() != state
 149:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(position.getProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(), rebaseStartedAt)
 150:                 || (position.getProfitLockActivatedAt() == null && activatedAt != null);
 151: 
 152:         if (changed) {
 153:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(highest);
 154:             position.setProfitLockActive(persistedActive);
 155:             position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(lockPrice);
 156:             position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 157:             position.setProfitLockActivatedAt(activatedAt);
 158:             position.setProfitLockState(state);
 159:             position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(rebaseStartedAt);
 160:             position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 161:             positionRepository.save(position);
 162:         }
 163: 
 164:         BigDecimal hardProfitFloor = entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 165:         boolean hardFloorTriggered = state == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 166:                 && current.compareTo(hardProfitFloor) < 0;
 167: 
 168:         if (changed || triggered || hardFloorTriggered || previousState != state) {
 169:             log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][PROFIT_LOCK_EVAL] positionId={}, symbol={}, current={}, entry={}, target={}, " +
 170:                             "previousHighest={}, highest={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousPersistedActive={}, persistedActive={}, " +
 171:                             "previousLock={}, lock={}, previousProgressPct={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, triggered={}, " +
 172:                             "hardFloor={}, hardFloorTriggered={}, persisted={}",
 173:                     position.getId(), symbol, current, entry, target,
 174:                     previousHighest, highest, previousState, state, previousPersistedActive, persistedActive,
 175:                     previousLock, lockPrice, previousProgress, progress, activation, triggered,
 176:                     hardProfitFloor, hardFloorTriggered, changed);
 177:         }
 178: 
 179:         String profileText = " Admin profile=" + profile.name() +
 180:                 " (activation=" + activation.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString() + "%, initial lock=" +
---LIVE PROTECTION 1-180---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.audit.service.ProductionExitAuditService;
   4: import com.crypto.position.domain.PositionManagementEvent;
   5: import com.crypto.position.repository.PositionManagementEventRepository;
   6: 
   7: import com.crypto.domain.PaperPosition;
   8: import com.crypto.domain.PositionStatus;
   9: import com.crypto.repository.PaperPositionRepository;
  10: import com.crypto.repository.TradeSignalRepository;

  13: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
  14: import com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletAutoExecutionService;
  15: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  16: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
  17: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

  20: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  21: import java.math.MathContext;

  24: 
  25: /**
  26:  * Mechanical protection for an already-open position using live Binance prices.
  27:  * TP / SL / Profit Lock must not wait for the next analysis signal.
  28:  */
  29: @Service
  30: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  31: @Slf4j
  32: public class LivePositionProtectionService {
  33:     // FIX-125 mandatory in Spring; null only in existing constructor-only policy tests.
  34:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  35:     private com.crypto.wallet.service.WalletTransactionCoordination walletCoordination;
  36: 
  37:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  38: 
  39:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
  40:     private final PaperPositionRepository paperPositionRepository;
  41:     private final DynamicProfitLockService dynamicProfitLockService;
  42:     private final PositionContinuationPolicy continuationPolicy;
  43:     private final PositionExitPolicy exitPolicy;
  44:     private final TradeSignalRepository tradeSignalRepository;
  45:     private final WalletAutoExecutionService walletAutoExecutionService;
  46:     private final ProductionExitAuditService productionExitAuditService;
  47:     private final PositionManagementEventRepository positionManagementEventRepository;
  48:     private final NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy;
  49: 
  50:     // FIX-153: direct observations use atomic effects+outcome; shared callers retain their ledger API.
  51:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired private com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService priceEvents;
  52:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private com.crypto.local.ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  53:     @Transactional(isolation=org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Isolation.READ_COMMITTED)
  54:     public void onPrice(String symbol,BigDecimal price,Instant observedAt,long eventId){
  55:         price=com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService.normalizePrice(price);
  56:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  57:         // Acquire the existing managed-position lock BEFORE freshness, including waits on it.
  58:         try {managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol,"OPEN");}
  59:         catch(RuntimeException failure){
  60:             // FIX-153: preserve only the established pre-business position-deadlock retry permission.
  61:             if(com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure))
  62:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  63:             throw failure;
  64:         }
  65:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();
  66:         String rejection=priceEvents.invalidReason(price,observedAt,exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow));
  67:         if(rejection==null && priceEvents.laterApplied(symbol,observedAt))rejection="OUT_OF_ORDER";
  68:         if(rejection!=null){priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"NON_CANONICAL",rejection);return;}
  69:         onPrice(symbol,price);
  70:         // No-position and all successful no-op exits reach this statement; exceptions do not.
  71:         priceEvents.finish(eventId,symbol,observedAt,price,"APPLIED","PROTECTION_CALLBACK_COMPLETED");
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     @Transactional
  75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {
  76:         if (symbolValue == null || symbolValue.isBlank() || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) return;
  77:         String symbol = symbolValue.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  78: 
  79:         // FIX-124: classify ONLY the first lock failure. The caller may retry after
  80:         // rollback in a fresh transaction; failures after evaluation are not retried.
  81:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.symbol(symbol);
  82:         WalletManagedPosition managed;
  83:         try {
  84:             managed = managedPositionRepository
  85:                     .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  86:                     .orElse(null);
  87:         } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
  88:             if (com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock.isMysqlDeadlock(failure)) {
  89:                 throw new com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction.InitialPositionLockDeadlock(failure);
  90:             }
  91:             throw failure;
  92:         }
  93:         if (managed == null || managed.getQuantity() == null || managed.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
  94:

 100:         if (managed.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && price.compareTo(managed.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {

 102:             // PositionManagementService now evaluate the same deterioration pressure (FIX-011).
 103:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(one, five, hour,

 111:                 Instant changedAt = Instant.now();
 112: 
 113:                 // FIX-118 behavioral phase: if Profit Lock was already earned under the old TP
 114:                 // but the wider approved target moves current progress back below activation,

 116:                 // lock remains stored but is non-executable until new-geometry activation is
 117:                 // earned again.
 118:                 DynamicProfitLockService.ExtensionTransition fix118Transition =
 119:                         dynamicProfitLockService.onTakeProfitExtended(managed, newTarget, price, changedAt);
 120:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][TP_EXTENSION] positionId={}, symbol={}, marketPrice={}, entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, " +
 121:                                 "highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 122:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}, activationPct={}",
 123:                         managed.getId(), symbol, price, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 124:                         managed.getHighestPriceUsdt(), managed.isProfitLockActive(), managed.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 125:                         fix118Transition.previousState(), fix118Transition.state(), fix118Transition.progressPercent(),

 131:                 resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(managed);
 132:                 managed.setUpdatedAt(changedAt);
 133:                 managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 134: 
 135:                 // FIX-067: Production has two persisted views of the same open lifecycle:
 136:                 // wallet_managed_position drives live protection, while paper_position is also
 137:                 // evaluated when a fresh TradeSignal arrives.  Before this fix only the managed
 138:                 // position received a TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED update.  The next 1m signal could

 208: 
 209:     /**
 210:      * FIX-120 correction/refinement to FIX-118. Called in the same transaction that persists the new TP.
 211:      * A TP change invalidates the prior executable lock as a calculation baseline. If current
 212:      * best progress remains at/above activation, stay ACTIVE but derive a fresh lock from the
 213:      * new geometry using raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as the historical
 214:      * best seed. If progress falls below activation, preserve the historical lock for audit but
 215:      * make it non-executable in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE until the current geometry requalifies.
 216:      */
 217:     public ExtensionTransition onTakeProfitExtended(WalletManagedPosition position, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant changedAt) {
 218:         if (position == null || newTarget == null || position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt() == null) {
 219:             return ExtensionTransition.none();
 220:         }
 221:         ProfitLockState previousState = normalizedState(position);
 222:         BigDecimal oldTarget = position.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 223:         BigDecimal oldLock = position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt();
 224:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = position.getHighestPriceUsdt() == null
 225:                 ? position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt()
 226:                 : position.getHighestPriceUsdt();
 227:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) {
 228:             extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 229:             position.setHighestPriceUsdt(extensionHighest);
 230:         }
 231:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 232:         BigDecimal activation = configuredProfile(settings()).activationPercent();
 233:         ProfitLockState nextState = previousState;
 234:         // TP changed now, so persist progress against the new geometry immediately even though
 235:         // the extension tick returns before the next normal Profit Lock evaluation.
 236:         position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(progress);
 237: 
 238:         if (previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 239:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 240:                 nextState = ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 241:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 242:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt);
 243:                 // Legacy compatibility flag deliberately stays true; ProfitLockState is the sole
 244:                 // authority for whether the persisted historical lock may execute.
 245:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 246:             } else {
 247:                 // FIX-120: the extension still leaves the winner beyond activation, but the old
 248:                 // lock belongs to the superseded TP geometry. Establish a fresh geometry baseline.
 249:                 // Deliberately pass raw extensionPrice as current and extensionHighest as previous
 250:                 // highest: ProfitLockPolicy then sees the real boundary tick plus the authoritative
 251:                 // best price, while previousActive/previousLock are intentionally discarded.
 252:                 ProfitLockProfile profile = configuredProfile(settings());
 253:                 BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 254:                 ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 255:                         position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 256:                         false, null, true, profile.activationPercent(), profile.initialLockPercent(), profile.trailStepPercent());
 257:                 nextState = rebased.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 258:                 position.setHighestPriceUsdt(rebased.highestPrice());
 259:                 position.setProfitLockProgressPercent(rebased.progressPercent());
 260:                 position.setProfitLockPriceUsdt(rebased.lockPrice());
 261:                 position.setProfitLockState(nextState);
 262:                 position.setProfitLockActive(true);
 263:                 position.setProfitLockRebaseStartedAt(nextState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 264:                         ? (changedAt == null ? Instant.now() : changedAt)
 265:                         : null);
 266:                 progress = rebased.progressPercent();
 267:             }
 268:         } else if (previousState == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 269:             // Repeated TP extensions keep the rebase active and refresh progress against the
 270:             // newest geometry without resetting the original rebase start timestamp.
 271:         }
 272: 
 273:         log.info("[FIX-120][PRODUCTION][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, symbol={}, previousState={}, newState={}, " +
 274:                         "oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, " +
 275:                         "activationPct={}, geometryRebased={}",
 276:                 position.getId(), position.getSymbol(), previousState, nextState, oldTarget, newTarget,
 277:                 extensionPrice, position.getHighestPriceUsdt(), progress,
 278:                 oldLock, position.getProfitLockPriceUsdt(),
 279:                 activation, previousState == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE);
 280: 
 281:         return new ExtensionTransition(previousState, nextState, progress, activation);
 282:     }
 283: 
 284:     private ProfitLockState normalizedState(WalletManagedPosition position) {
 285:         if (position.getProfitLockState() != null) return position.getProfitLockState();
 286:         return position.isProfitLockActive() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 287:     }
 288: 
 289:     private BigDecimal progressPercent(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal highest) {
 290:         if (entry == null || target == null || highest == null || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 291:         BigDecimal favorable = highest.subtract(entry);
 292:         if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 293:         return favorable.multiply(HUNDRED)
 294:                 .divide(target.subtract(entry), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 295:     }

 298:     public boolean isActive(String symbol) {
 299:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) return false;
 300:         return positionRepository.findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(
 301:                         symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT), "OPEN")
 302:                 .map(p -> normalizedState(p) == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE)
 303:                 .orElse(false);
 304:     }

 352:     private boolean different(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
 353:         if (a == null && b == null) return false;
 354:         if (a == null || b == null) return true;
 355:         return a.compareTo(b) != 0;
 356:     }

 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
 271:                 || !sameDecimal(managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), next.bestPrice())
 272:                 || managed.getNearTpBearishStreak() != next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute()
 273:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 274:         if (stateChanged) {
 275:             managed.setNearTpState(next.nearTpState());
 276:             managed.setNearTpBestPrice(next.bestPrice());
 277:             managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute());
 278:             managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 279:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 280:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 281:         }
 282: 
 283:         if (evaluation.transition() || stateChanged) {
 284:             log.info("FIX-11T Near-TP evaluation: positionId={}, symbol={}, state={}, code={}, price={}, bestPrice={}, " +
 285:                             "tpProgressPct={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, oneMinuteId={}, oneMinuteOriginal={}, " +
 286:                             "fiveMinuteId={}, fiveMinuteOriginal={}, detail={}",
 287:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), next.nearTpState(), evaluation.code(), price, next.bestPrice(),
 288:                     evaluation.tpProgressPercent(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(),
 289:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getId(),
 290:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 291:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getId(),
 292:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 293:                     evaluation.explanation());
 294:         } else if (log.isDebugEnabled() && evaluation.code().startsWith("HOLD_")) {
 295:             log.debug("FIX-11T Near-TP hold: positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, price={}, detail={}",
 296:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 297:         }
 298: 
 299:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return;
 300: 
 301:         WalletAutoExecutionService.PartialHarvestResult harvest = walletAutoExecutionService
 302:                 .executeNearTpPartialHarvest(managed.getSymbol(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 303:         if (!harvest.executed()) {
 304:             log.warn("FIX-11T Near-TP harvest not executed: positionId={}, symbol={}, status={}, price={}",
 305:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.status(), price);
 306:             // If execution did not happen, return to rejection monitoring rather than leaving
 307:             // the state stranded in FAILURE_CONFIRMED. Idempotency still lives in wallet_trade.
 308:             managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED);
 309:             if ("BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM".equals(harvest.status())
 310:                     || "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE".equals(harvest.status())) {
 311:                 // The skipped order consumed the current confirmation pair. Requiring fresh
 312:                 // bearish evidence prevents a retry on every subsequent market-price tick.
 313:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 314:             }
 315:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 316:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 317:             return;
 318:         }
 319: 
 320:         // Keep paper_position quantity/P&L synchronized with the managed wallet lifecycle.
 321:         // Without this, a later terminal signal close would calculate P&L on pre-harvest quantity.
 322:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(managed.getSymbol(), PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 323:         if (paper != null) {
 324:             paper.setQuantity(harvest.remainingQuantity());
 325:             BigDecimal priorRealized = paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl();
 326:             paper.setRealizedPnl(priorRealized.add(harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), MC));
 327:             paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 328:         }
 329: 
 330:         log.info("FIX-11T NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, " +
 331:                         "price={}, realizedPnl={}, realizedPnlPct={}",
 332:                 managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.soldQuantity(), harvest.remainingQuantity(),
 333:                 price, harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), harvest.realizedPnlPercent());
 334:     }
 335: 
 336:     private TradeSignal latestEligibleSignal(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal newest, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 337:         if (newest != null && newest.getGeneratedAt() != null && !newest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) {
 338:             return newest;
 339:         }
 340:         return tradeSignalRepository
 341:                 .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, evaluatedAt)
 342:                 .orElse(null);
 343:     }
 344: 
 345:     /**
 346:      * FIX-117: TP extension must not erase Near-TP protection that was already earned.
 347:      *
 348:      * Only ARMED and REJECTION_DETECTED change from the pre-FIX-117 behavior. INACTIVE
 349:      * still resets against the new geometry. FAILURE_CONFIRMED also keeps the existing
 350:      * reset behavior because that persisted/transient lifecycle has a real interruption
 351:      * window between state save and harvest resolution and is intentionally out of scope.
 352:      * PARTIAL_HARVESTED remains terminal through the existing harvest-used guard.
 353:      *
 354:      * plannedDistance is recalculated from the current TP by NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy
 355:      * on every evaluation, so the historical best price remains a valid market observation
 356:      * and does not need synthetic rebasing after an extension.
 357:      */
 358:     private void resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(WalletManagedPosition managed) {
 359:         if (managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) return;
 360: 
 361:         NearTpState currentState = managed.getNearTpState() == null
 362:                 ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState();
 363:         switch (currentState) {
 364:             case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
 365:                 // Existing behavior: no earned protection is carried into the new geometry.
 366:                 managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.INACTIVE);
 367:                 managed.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
 368:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 369:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 370:             }
 371:             case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> {
 372:                 // FIX-117: preserve earned protection and the real historical best price.
 373:             }
 374:             case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
 375:                 // Preserve earned protection/best price, but discard bearish persistence
 376:                 // accumulated under the old, tighter geometry. The next evaluation will
 377:                 // recalculate giveback against the new TP and either recover or remain rejected.
 378:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 379:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 380:             }
 381:             case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> {
 382:                 // Existing terminal behavior; normally returned above by nearTpHarvestUsed.
 383:             }
 384:         }
 385:     }
 386: 
 387:     private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
 388:         return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
 389:     }
 390: 
 391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
 392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
 393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 394:         if (paper == null) return;
 395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
 396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
 397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
 398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
 399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 402:         paper.setStatus(status);
 403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
 405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 407: 
 408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
 409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
 410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
 411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 412:     }
 413: }
---PROFIT LOCK POLICY---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   4: 
   5: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   6: import java.math.RoundingMode;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * Pure Dynamic Profit Lock policy shared by live production and Proven/Regression.
  10:  * Keep all percentage/progression math here so replay can never drift from production.
  11:  */
  12: @Component
  13: public class ProfitLockPolicy {
  14:     private static final BigDecimal HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  15:     private static final int SCALE = 12;
  16: 
  17:     public State evaluate(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal current,
  18:                           BigDecimal previousHighest, boolean previouslyActive, BigDecimal previousLock,
  19:                           boolean enabled, BigDecimal activationPercent, BigDecimal initialLockPercent,
  20:                           BigDecimal trailStepPercent) {
  21:         if (entry == null || target == null || current == null || entry.signum() <= 0 || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) {
  22:             return new State(previousHighest, previouslyActive, previousLock, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  23:         }
  24: 
  25:         BigDecimal highest = previousHighest;
  26:         if (highest == null || highest.compareTo(entry) < 0) highest = entry;
  27:         if (current.compareTo(highest) > 0) highest = current;
  28:         if (!enabled) return new State(highest, false, null, BigDecimal.ZERO, false);
  29: 
  30:         BigDecimal distance = target.subtract(entry);
  31:         BigDecimal progress = highest.subtract(entry).multiply(HUNDRED)
  32:                 .divide(distance, 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);
  33:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(activationPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
  34:         BigDecimal initial = nvl(initialLockPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(40));
  35:         BigDecimal step = nvl(trailStepPercent, BigDecimal.valueOf(10));
  36: 
  37:         boolean active = previouslyActive;
  38:         BigDecimal lock = previousLock;
  39:         if (progress.compareTo(activation) >= 0) {
  40:             active = true;
  41:             BigDecimal completed = progress.subtract(activation).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  42:                     .divide(step, 0, RoundingMode.DOWN);
  43:             BigDecimal lockedProgress = initial.add(completed.multiply(step));
  44:             BigDecimal maximum = progress.subtract(step).max(initial);
  45:             lockedProgress = lockedProgress.min(maximum).max(initial);
  46:             BigDecimal candidate = entry.add(distance.multiply(lockedProgress)
  47:                     .divide(HUNDRED, SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
  48:             candidate = candidate.max(entry.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005)));
  49:             if (lock == null || candidate.compareTo(lock) > 0) lock = candidate;
  50:         }
  51:         boolean triggered = active && lock != null && current.compareTo(lock) <= 0;
  52:         return new State(highest, active, lock, progress, triggered);
  53:     }
  54: 
  55:     private static BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) {
  56:         return value == null ? fallback : value;
  57:     }
  58: 
  59:     public record State(BigDecimal highestPrice, boolean active, BigDecimal lockPrice,
  60:                         BigDecimal progressPercent, boolean triggered) {}
  61: }
---THESIS PRESSURE---
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   5: 
   6: /**
   7:  * Shared immutable-BUY-thesis deterioration policy.
   8:  *
   9:  * Production position analysis and take-profit continuation MUST use the same
  10:  * deterioration definition. This prevents one component from saying HOLD while
  11:  * another closes the same position because of a different hard-coded trend floor.
  12:  *
  13:  * The policy compares the immutable entry thesis with the current TradeSignal.
  14:  * It does not change signal generation, BUY/SELL decisions, ATR entry logic or
  15:  * higher-timeframe vetoes.
  16:  */
  17: @Component
  18: public class PositionThesisPressurePolicy {
  19: 
  20:     public ThesisPressure evaluate(Integer entryTrend,
  21:                                    Integer entryStructure,
  22:                                    Integer entryMomentum,
  23:                                    Integer entryVolume,
  24:                                    Integer entryConfidence,
  25:                                    Integer entryTotal,
  26:                                    TradeSignal current) {
  27:         if (current == null) {
  28:             return new ThesisPressure(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
  29:                     0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
  30:                     0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
  31:                     8, 5);
  32:         }
  33: 
  34:         int eTrend = valueOrCurrent(entryTrend, current.getTrendScore());
  35:         int eStructure = valueOrCurrent(entryStructure, current.getTrendStructureScore());
  36:         int eMomentum = valueOrCurrent(entryMomentum, current.getMomentumScore());
  37:         int eVolume = valueOrCurrent(entryVolume, current.getVolumeScore());
  38:         int eConfidence = valueOrCurrent(entryConfidence, current.getConfidenceScore());
  39:         int eTotal = valueOrCurrent(entryTotal, current.getTotalScore());
  40: 
  41:         int trendDrop = positiveDrop(eTrend, current.getTrendScore());
  42:         int structureDrop = positiveDrop(eStructure, current.getTrendStructureScore());
  43:         int momentumDrop = positiveDrop(eMomentum, current.getMomentumScore());
  44:         int volumeDrop = positiveDrop(eVolume, current.getVolumeScore());
  45:         int confidenceDrop = positiveDrop(eConfidence, current.getConfidenceScore());
  46:         int totalDrop = positiveDrop(eTotal, current.getTotalScore());
  47: 
  48:         // Keep these thresholds identical to the historically proven
  49:         // PositionManagementService immutable-thesis calculation.
  50:         int trendPressure = Math.min(8,
  51:                 points(trendDrop, 3, 6, 10, 3)
  52:                         + points(structureDrop, 2, 3, 5, 2)
  53:                         + (current.getTrendScore() <= 8 ? 2 : current.getTrendScore() <= 13 ? 1 : 0));
  54: 
  55:         int momentumPressure = Math.min(5,
  56:                 points(momentumDrop, 3, 5, 8, 2)
  57:                         + (current.getMomentumScore() <= 4 ? 2 : current.getMomentumScore() <= 8 ? 1 : 0));
  58: 
  59:         return new ThesisPressure(
  60:                 eTrend, current.getTrendScore(), trendDrop,
  61:                 eStructure, current.getTrendStructureScore(), structureDrop,
  62:                 eMomentum, current.getMomentumScore(), momentumDrop,
  63:                 eVolume, current.getVolumeScore(), volumeDrop,
  64:                 eConfidence, current.getConfidenceScore(), confidenceDrop,
  65:                 eTotal, current.getTotalScore(), totalDrop,
  66:                 trendPressure, momentumPressure);
  67:     }
  68: 
  69:     private int valueOrCurrent(Integer entryValue, int currentValue) {
  70:         return entryValue == null ? currentValue : entryValue;
  71:     }
  72: 
  73:     private int positiveDrop(int entry, int current) {
  74:         return Math.max(0, entry - current);
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     private int points(int drop, int low, int medium, int high, int highPoints) {
  78:         if (drop >= high) return highPoints;
  79:         if (drop >= medium) return Math.max(1, highPoints - 1);
  80:         if (drop >= low) return 1;
  81:         return 0;
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     public record ThesisPressure(
  85:             int entryTrend, int currentTrend, int trendDrop,
  86:             int entryStructure, int currentStructure, int structureDrop,
  87:             int entryMomentum, int currentMomentum, int momentumDrop,
  88:             int entryVolume, int currentVolume, int volumeDrop,
  89:             int entryConfidence, int currentConfidence, int confidenceDrop,
  90:             int entryTotal, int currentTotal, int totalDrop,
  91:             int trendPressure, int momentumPressure) {
  92:     }
  93: }

   5: Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
   6: happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
   7: rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
   8: at that time.
   9: 
  10: Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
  11: database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.
  12: 
  13: ## Required input
  14: 
  15: Obtain or resolve:
  16:

  20:   a bounded approximate entry-time window.
  21: - Optional question or suspected issue.
  22: 
  23: Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
  24: level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
  25: columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
  26: `crypto_ai_v2`.
  27: 
  28: ## Evidence rules
  29: 
  30: - Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
  31: - Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
  32: - Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
  33: - Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
  34: - Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  35:   conflicting evidence**.
  36: - Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
  37: - A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  38:   transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  39:   committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  40:   no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
  41: - Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  42:   no configuration history exists, report that limitation.
  43: 
  44: ## Investigation workflow

  46: ### 1. Resolve identity and environment
  47: 
  48: Link the trade reference to, where available:
  49: 
  50: - `paper_position`
  51: - `wallet_trade`
  52: - entry and exit `trade_signal`
  53: - `signal_processing_work`
  54: - wallet/position execution records
  55:

  57: local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.
  58: 
  59: ### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

  62: requires it. Collect:
  63:

  69: | `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
  70: | `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
  71: | `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
  72: | configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
  73: | application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |
  74: 
  75: Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.
  76:

  78:

  80:

  82:

  84:

  86: - signal generation to processing/entry;
  87: - observation time to protection completion;
  88: - exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.
  89: 
  90: Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
  91: or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.
  92: 
  93: ### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality
  94: 
  95: Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:
  96:

 105:

 146:

---INVESTIGATION LANE---
   1: # Trade Investigation Agent
   2: 
   3: ## Role
   4: 
   5: Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
   6: happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
   7: rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
   8: at that time.
   9: 
  10: Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
  11: database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.
  12: 
  13: ## Required input
  14: 
  15: Obtain or resolve:
  16: 
  17: - Environment and application: local, test, or production.
  18: - Database host, port, and schema.
  19: - One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  20:   a bounded approximate entry-time window.
  21: - Optional question or suspected issue.
  22: 
  23: Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
  24: level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
  25: columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
  26: `crypto_ai_v2`.
  27: 
  28: ## Evidence rules
  29: 
  30: - Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
  31: - Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
  32: - Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
  33: - Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
  34: - Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  35:   conflicting evidence**.
  36: - Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
  37: - A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  38:   transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  39:   committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  40:   no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
  41: - Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  42:   no configuration history exists, report that limitation.
  43: 
  44: ## Investigation workflow
  45: 
  46: ### 1. Resolve identity and environment
  47: 
  48: Link the trade reference to, where available:
  49: 
  50: - `paper_position`
  51: - `wallet_trade`
  52: - entry and exit `trade_signal`
  53: - `signal_processing_work`
  54: - wallet/position execution records
  55: 
  56: Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
  57: local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.
  58: 
  59: ### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle
  60: 
  61: Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
  62: requires it. Collect:
  63: 
  64: | Evidence | Establishes |
  65: | --- | --- |
  66: | `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
  67: | `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
  68: | `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
  69: | `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
  70: | `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
  71: | `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
  72: | configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
  73: | application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |
  74: 
  75: Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.
  76: 
  77: ### 3. Reconstruct the timeline
  78: 
  79: Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:
  80: 
  81: `candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`
  82: 
  83: Measure separately:
  84: 
  85: - candle close to signal generation;
  86: - signal generation to processing/entry;
  87: - observation time to protection completion;
  88: - exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.
  89: 
  90: Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
  91: or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.
  92: 
  93: ### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality
  94: 
  95: Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:
  96: 
  97: - signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
  98: - final decision and entry-allowed result;
  99: - BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
 100:   strategy gates that applied at that time;
 101: - correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
 102: - quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.
 103: 
 104: Return two separate conclusions:
 105: 
 106: - **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
 107: - **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
 108:   evidence.
 109: 
 110: Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
 111: as information the entry decision had.
 112: 
 113: ### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior
 114: 
 115: For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
 116: `PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
 117: gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
 118: evidence.
 119: 
 120: For each relevant accepted observation:
 121: 
 122: 1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
 123: 2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
 124: 3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
 125:    wallet execution record.
 126: 4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
 127:    lock waits, or pool pressure.
 128: 
 129: Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
 130: take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.
 131: 
 132: ### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality
 133: 
 134: Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
 135: Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
 136: the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
 137: the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.
 138: 
 139: ### 7. Reconcile financial results
 140: 
 141: Recalculate using execution records:
 142: 
 143: - bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
 144: - gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
 145: - partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.
 146: 
 147: Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.
 148: 
 149: ### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully
 150: 
 151: Classify findings as one or more of:
 152: 
 153: - strategy decision;
 154: - data freshness or coverage;
 155: - analysis delay;
 156: - execution failure;
 157: - protection failure;
 158: - database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
 159: - accounting/UI discrepancy;
 160: - insufficient evidence.
 161: 
 162: Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
 163: prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
 164: material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
 165: price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.
 166: 
 167: ## Required final report
 168: 
 169: 1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
 170: 2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
 171: 3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
 172: 4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
 173: 5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
 174: 6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
 175: 7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
 176:    issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
 177: 8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
 178:    references.
 179: 9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
 180: 10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.
 181: 
 182: A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
 183: execution. State those independently.

.\agent-docs\runs\20261009-185153\round05-codex.stderr.txt-7334-md\FIX-136.md:7:LIVE and Replay explicitly pass the application's local transaction manager to the audit scope. Each audit INSERT executes in a writable REQUIRES_NEW transaction, suspending and restoring the outer transaction and its connection. Evidence commits independently and can remain after the caller fails: an audit row proves inputs were read, not that analysis or execution completed. Hash format and input order are unchanged. Errors propagate; they are never swallowed to allow unaudited execution. The legacy two-argument scope overload remains for standalone JDBC tests; production JPA callers use the three-argument overload.
.\agent-docs\runs\20261009-185153\round05-codex.stderr.txt-7335-md\FIX-136.md:9:The collector reader stays read-only. No writes go to collector tables. Audit transactions use the existing local pool and borrow an additional connection while the outer connection is suspended. SQL timeout is five seconds; pool checkout retains its existing 30-second limit (not an end-to-end deadline). Pool contention remains possible under saturation and must be observed; this patch does not solve shared-reader query timeouts or promise zero latency. Do not increase worker concurrency without measuring local pool headroom.
.\agent-docs\runs\20261009-185153\round05-codex.stderr.txt:7336:md\FIX-136.md:11:Local Hikari connections explicitly set UTC at both JDBC and MySQL session levels. `serverTimezone=UTC` alone does not force the server session timezone. Hibernate's existing UTC setting is retained. This affects future local reads/writes, so persisted timestamps must be reconciled before enabling the corrected consumer against prior shifted state. No global MySQL or collector timezone changes.

.\md\FIX-136.md-9-The collector reader stays read-only. No writes go to collector tables. Audit transactions use the existing local pool and borrow an additional connection while the outer connection is suspended. SQL timeout is five seconds; pool checkout retains its existing 30-second limit (not an end-to-end deadline). Pool contention remains possible under saturation and must be observed; this patch does not solve shared-reader query timeouts or promise zero latency. Do not increase worker concurrency without measuring local pool headroom.
.\md\FIX-136.md-10-
.\md\FIX-136.md:11:Local Hikari connections explicitly set UTC at both JDBC and MySQL session levels. `serverTimezone=UTC` alone does not force the server session timezone. Hibernate's existing UTC setting is retained. This affects future local reads/writes, so persisted timestamps must be reconciled before enabling the corrected consumer against prior shifted state. No global MySQL or collector timezone changes.
.\md\FIX-136.md-12-
.\md\FIX-136.md-13-Worker failures now retain an exception summary in the delivery row, without resetting owner tokens or phases. An audit-specific log includes context and read index. Existing scoring, thresholds, eligibility checks, wallet coordination, and Replay business rules are unchanged.

---EXIT AUDIT SOURCE---
   1: package com.crypto.audit.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.audit.domain.ProductionExitAudit;
   4: import com.crypto.audit.repository.ProductionExitAuditRepository;
   5: import com.crypto.domain.PaperPosition;
   6: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   7: import com.crypto.position.domain.PositionAnalysis;
   8: import com.crypto.position.repository.PositionAnalysisRepository;
   9: import com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletManagedPosition;
  10: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletManagedPositionRepository;
  11: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  13: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  14: 
  15: import java.time.Instant;
  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * FIX-028: records the actual production exit cause without changing execution.
  19:  *
  20:  * Example that motivated the audit: BTC hit TAKE_PROFIT while the latest 1m signal
  21:  * was WATCH. The legacy wallet ledger called that SIGNAL_SELL because it reused the
  22:  * current signal as an execution carrier. This service preserves both facts:
  23:  * closeTrigger=TAKE_PROFIT and sourceSignalDecision=WATCH.
  24:  */
  25: @Service
  26: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  27: public class ProductionExitAuditService {
  28:     private final ProductionExitAuditRepository auditRepository;
  29:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
  30:     private final PositionAnalysisRepository positionAnalysisRepository;
  31: 
  32:     @Transactional
  33:     public void record(PaperPosition paper, TradeSignal sourceSignal, String closeTrigger, String explanation) {
  34:         if (paper == null || paper.getId() == null || paper.getExitPrice() == null || closeTrigger == null) return;
  35:         if (auditRepository.findTopByPaperPositionIdOrderByAuditedAtDesc(paper.getId()).isPresent()) return;
  36: 
  37:         WalletManagedPosition managed = paper.getSignal() == null ? null : managedPositionRepository
  38:                 .findTopByEntrySignalIdOrderByOpenedAtDesc(paper.getSignal().getId()).orElse(null);
  39:         PositionAnalysis analysis = managed == null || managed.getId() == null ? null : positionAnalysisRepository
  40:                 .findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(managed.getId()).orElse(null);
  41: 
  42:         auditRepository.save(ProductionExitAudit.builder()
  43:                 .paperPositionId(paper.getId())
  44:                 .walletPositionId(managed == null ? null : managed.getId())
  45:                 .symbol(paper.getSymbol())
  46:                 .closeTrigger(closeTrigger)
  47:                 .sourceSignalId(sourceSignal == null ? null : sourceSignal.getId())
  48:                 .sourceSignalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getDecision().name())
  49:                 .sourceSignalOriginalDecision(sourceSignal == null || sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : sourceSignal.getOriginalDecision().name())
  50:                 .positionAnalysisId(analysis == null ? null : analysis.getId())
  51:                 .positionRecommendation(analysis == null || analysis.getRecommendation() == null ? null : analysis.getRecommendation().name())
  52:                 .entryPriceUsdt(paper.getEntryPrice())
  53:                 .exitPriceUsdt(paper.getExitPrice())
  54:                 .stopLossUsdt(paper.getStopLoss())
  55:                 .takeProfitUsdt(paper.getTakeProfit())
  56:                 .closeExplanation(explanation)
  57:                 .auditedAt(Instant.now())
  58:                 .build());
  59:     }
  60: }
